# Feature & Product Decisions
> Requires: CLAUDE.md

Resolved product, feature scope, business rules, compliance, and UX decisions. Load this when building a specific feature to understand what was decided and why.

For architecture and stack decisions see `plan/docs/DECISIONS-ARCH.md`.

## Current Feature Addenda

| ID | Decision |
|---|---|
| CAL-01 | **Calendar bulk room/date actions + hard staff inventory hold (owner-requested 2026-10-12; implemented on `codex/calendar-bulk-holds`):** the admin booking calendar selects one contiguous date range across one or more physical rooms. Both outcomes require a confirmation modal that names the rooms, dates, nights, and room-night total. **Block dates** writes one `roomBlocks` doc per room in a single transaction under a shared `batchId`; any conflict aborts the whole batch. **Hold & book** creates a separate 15-minute `staffInventoryHolds/{id}` hard lock, then opens the existing multi-room New Booking modal with rooms/dates fixed. Public availability and all authoritative booking, reschedule, and block conflict paths consult unexpired staff holds. The creating staff member may extend or release; walk-in creation validates the hold owner, exact room set, and exact dates, excludes only that hold from conflict detection, and consumes it in the same transaction that writes the reservation. Closing the modal releases the hold, but submission locks modal close/release until the transaction resolves. Expiry is authoritative at read time; the existing daily hold sweep only normalizes stale statuses. The public `bookingHolds` countdown remains a separate soft signal and is not reused. All APIs stay inside the existing catch-all Vercel function. Required Firestore rules permit staff reads and deny all client writes; composite indexes cover active range, room membership, and expiry queries. |
| EC-03 | **Admin-granted early check-in from the booking drawer (owner-requested 2026-08-22; implemented on `feature/admin-grant-early-checkin`):** administrators can add early check-in directly to an upcoming booking without requiring a guest request first. Creation is limited to bookings with no existing early-check-in record, status `payment-confirmed` or `confirmed`, and a check-in date that has not passed. The server re-checks the Admin role; Front Desk can still resolve guest-originated requests but sees staff grants read-only. The optional `earlyCheckIn.source` discriminator is `guest-request` or `staff-granted`, with an absent legacy value treated as a guest request. The existing resolve endpoint accepts grant mode, uses a Firestore transaction, refuses to overwrite existing data, and makes exact retries idempotent without duplicate guest email. Staff-granted email and Rewards copy say the perk was added to the stay. The drawer collects a required time and optional guest-facing note, then reuses the existing approved calendar icon and drawer-header badge. No new Vercel function. |
| BDUX-06 | **Booking drawer content hierarchy and de-duplication (owner-requested 2026-08-22; implemented on `feature/rearrange-booking-drawer`):** preserve the four-tab workspace but give each detail one authoritative home. The global header remains the scan surface for guest/stay/payment context and compact money state. Overview is reduced to arrival-related early check-in work followed by special requests. Check-in retains readiness, registration, guest ID, government-discount verification, and breakfast selections. Folio is the only detailed payment-proof/ledger/discount/receipt surface. Activity & More presents booking mutations before email resend/history. Removed duplicates: the second Stay & Accommodation card, the Overview payment-proof row, the Overview room-move trigger, and the pending proof's separate Open Full Size/Preview pair. Group-level reservation money and room-level header money remain intentionally distinct. No data model, API, permission, or lifecycle behavior changes. |
| SR-01 | **Guest booking Step 2 special-requests block — bed set-up + additional preferences + arrival time (owner-requested 2026-11-12; implemented on `feature/special-requests-bed-setup`, v1.7.2 → v1.8.0):** the public + corporate booking Step 2 had no in-flow preference surface after `feat/special-requests-redirect` (2026-08-21) retired the free-text field; guests who cared about the type now had to phone/email the desk post-booking. Owner asked for two surfaces: **(1) bed set-up selection (twin vs large bed) for Twin and Double room types**, configurable via the Room Types editor + a global bed set-ups catalog in Settings, AND **(2) a hidden "Show additional preferences" accordion** containing a checkbox grid of admin-configured preferences + a free-text "additional request" box + an "expected arrival time" dropdown. **Decisions.** **(1) Bed set-up is always visible when applicable, NOT a hidden accordion** — owner explicitly chose this UX shape. **(2) Per-type enablement, NOT global** — the Room Types editor carries a `bedSetupValues: string[]` subset of the global `bedSetups` catalog. Empty subset = "this type doesn't offer a choice" (the Step 2 selector is hidden). **(3) Additional preferences are admin-configurable in Settings → Booking Preferences** — a new tab between Room Types + Branding. CRUD on a `bookingPreferences: { value, label, icon?, surchargeNote? }[]` catalog. **(5) `surchargeNote` is informational only** — no per-preference price tag wiring. **(7) Free-text "additional request" re-introduces the guest-captured path; the `feat/special-requests-redirect` redirect card is retired**. The `Booking.specialRequests` staff-captured field stays for post-booking email/phone follow-ups — the two surfaces are independent and live side-by-side in the drawer. **(8) Expected arrival time = 24h window from `hotelConfig.checkInTime`, NOT admin-configurable** — owner explicitly chose "keep it fixed" 2026-11-12. Times earlier than check-in are NOT offered (the existing `earlyCheckIn` Step 4 flow covers that). **(9) Scope = both public `/book` AND `/corporate/book`**. **(10) Admin read surfaces**: new read-only "Guest special requests" block in the booking drawer above the staff `BookingSpecialRequestsEditor`; calendar grid amber dot now also gates on per-room `bedSetup`; staff new-booking email adds a `Bed set-up` row. **(11) Server cross-references** — every `bedSetup` is validated against the room type's `bedSetupValues` subset, every `additionalPreferences` key against `bookingPreferences[].value`, every `expectedArrivalTime` against `buildArrivalTimeOptions(hotelConfig.checkInTime)` (the same helper the guest dropdown uses). Invalid → 400. **(12) Wire shape**: `roomSelections[i].bedSetup`, `additionalPreferences: string[]`, `additionalRequest: string ≤500 chars`, `expectedArrivalTime: HH:MM | null`. **(13) Storage**: per-room `Booking.bedSetup: string \| null` + reservation-level `additionalPreferences` / `additionalRequest` / `expectedArrivalTime` on the header (server-validated, immutable snapshot). **(14) Smoking/non-smoking is out of scope** — owner explicitly excluded 2026-11-12. Full spec + decision rationale in `plan/features/SPECIAL-REQUESTS.md`. No new Vercel function. |
| VAT-01 | **Admin Tax (VAT) toggle — hide the 12% VAT breakdown across every product surface (owner-requested 2026-10-12; implemented on `feature/vat-toggle`, v1.8.0 → v1.9.0):** the BIR 12% VAT breakdown (decision #115 / #149 / #150) ships everywhere a bill appears — Reports + XLSX export, admin booking drawer, admin print-to-PDF receipt, guest `/book` live preview, guest confirmation + lookup pages. The hotel is a new business below the BIR VAT-registration threshold (currently ₱3M annual gross sales); the operator is NOT registered and so should not display "VATable Sales / VAT-Exempt Sales / VAT Amount" on the bill. Owner asked for an admin-controlled kill switch. **Decisions.** **(1) Single boolean toggle, NOT a rate override.** Two real-world states exist for any hotel: "VAT-registered" (12% PH standard) and "below the BIR threshold" (0%). A future non-PH deployment that needs a different rate can extend the type with a numeric `rate` field; the helper signature is forward-compatible. **(2) Default ON, hydrate byte-equivalent.** `settings/hotelConfig.vatConfig = { enabled: true }` is the BIR-compliant default; every existing hotel that has never set the field renders the 12% breakdown everywhere (zero behavior change for current deployments). **`normalizeVatConfig` in `AdminContext`'s `onSnapshot` merge** backfills the default for legacy `settings/hotelConfig` docs that predate the field — same pattern as `normalizeDiscountScope` / `normalizePaymentHoldWindowHours`. **(3) When OFF, the breakdown block disappears entirely — NOT a zero-line block.** The operator's published rate is the guest's net amount; reporting a zero VAT block would mislead the desk. The on-screen Reports section, the XLSX Summary sheet block, the XLSX per-booking VAT columns, the admin drawer sub-block, the admin print-to-PDF receipt section, and the guest live preview all consult `isVatEnabled(hotelConfig.vatConfig)` to decide whether to render — single helper, single source of truth. **(4) The tax math itself stays consistent.** `getEffectiveVatRate(vatConfig)` returns `0.12` when ON and `0` when OFF, and the per-booking `calculateVatBreakdown` helper accepts the rate override so the per-booking math (`vatExclusiveSales = totalPrice / 1.12` etc.) is byte-equivalent at 0% — the rendering is the only thing that changes. **(5) Lives on Settings → Discounts tab, NOT a new top-level tab.** The tab already carries BIR-cousin controls (Senior/PWD discount scope) and the VAT toggle is the same audience + same decision-making surface. Admin-only (same RA 9994-style guardrail as the discount-scope row); front-desk staff cannot reach the toggle. **(6) Surfaced to the guest app via the public site content hook** — `usePublicSiteContent().vatConfig` — so the live preview (`/book` Step 3) reads the same source of truth the admin drawer + Reports + XLSX read. **(7) No new Vercel function, no schema migration.** The toggle persists via the existing `updateSettings("hotelConfig", { vatConfig: { enabled: ... } })` call; legacy `settings/hotelConfig` docs hydrate via the same `normalizeVatConfig` backfill. **Verified.** `shared` 71/71 + new `admin-app` source-text test pinning 4-surface wiring + `tsc -p admin-app` + `tsc -p guest-app` all clean. No new Vercel function. |
| BR-01 | **Breakfast redesign — meal-based add-on with configurable cut-off + guest manageability + staff override (owner-requested 2026-10-12; spec on `feature/breakfast-redesign`; full brief in `plan/features/BREAKFAST.md`):** the current breakfast model is a flat-rate toggle with a per-date/per-guest `Record<key, string>` of meal names — a shape that fits one rate and one menu but not a boutique hotel that wants per-meal pricing, guest-side editability, a daily cut-off, kitchen handoff, and refund liability. Owner + client closed a 10-decision design conversation and approved the redesign as a Phase 13 feature. **Decisions. (1) Per-meal catalog with hotel-wide prices.** `settings/breakfastConfig.meals: BreakfastMeal[]` (was `silogItems[]`) gains `price: number`, `description: string`, `displayOrder: number`; the existing `id / name / isActive` fields are preserved. A meal with `isActive: false` cannot be reactivated to a different price if any booking snapshot references it (snapshots are immutable per decision 9). (2) **Configurable cut-off, default 8:00 PM, hotel timezone.** `settings/breakfastConfig.cutoffTime: "HH:MM"` (default `"20:00"`) + `cutoffTimezone: string` (defaults to `hotel.config.ts` timezone; falls back to `Asia/Manila` with a warning). The cut-off is `(serviceDate − 1 day) at cutoffTime in hotelTz` and applies to add, change, AND remove. **Server-side enforcement** — the existing catch-all `/api/bookings/*` routes gain a `computeBreakfastCutoffState` helper; the server is the source of truth. (3) **Per-guest, per-date granularity, exactly one meal per slot.** No "No meal" entry — leaving a slot empty means skip. Children and extra-bed occupants are ordinary meal slots (the previous `breakfastIncludesChildren` toggle is removed). (4) **Step 1 "Room + Breakfast" toggle removed.** The public + corporate + walk-in flows share the same shape: Step 1 is rooms + dates only; Step 2 gains a new "Breakfast" section with a per-date × per-guest meal grid; the sidebar updates live. (5) **Discounts apply per the existing scope matrix** (decision #146). Senior/PWD, voucher, Spark Rewards, and corporate rate all check the scope matrix at the time of pricing. The `allowOnlineSeniorPwdClaim` toggle is the gate for online Senior/PWD claims, same as for room rates. (6) **Always rolls into the running booking balance** — no separate online-payment moment for breakfast. Removing an unpaid meal reduces the amount due; removing a paid meal opens a refund liability. (7) **Guest manageability via `/my-booking/breakfast`** — new guest post-booking page reusing the existing `ref + lookupToken` magic-link auth. Cutoff countdown per date, locked dates read-only, served slots read-only. Batched `POST /api/bookings/breakfast/update` is idempotent on `clientBatchId`. (8) **Front-desk + admin override with required reason.** When a staff member changes a meal on a locked date, a confirmation modal collects a free-text reason (min 5 chars). The reason writes to the audit entry and surfaces in the kitchen list's "Late changes" column. The `frontDeskOverrideEnabled` setting is a defensive kill switch (default true). (9) **Snapshot immutability.** `breakfastSelections[key].mealId / mealName / price` are frozen at order time. Deleted menu items still show on existing bookings, tagged "(no longer offered)". (10) **Refund liability extends the existing refunds subcollection** — `OnsitePayment.category: "cancellation" \| "breakfast"` (NEW) + `breakfastRefundContext: { mealId, slotKey, mealName, priceSnapshot }` (NEW, optional). No new collection; the existing Refunds admin view gains a `category` filter chip; the dashboard gains a pending-refunds badge. (11) **Kitchen handoff at cut-off** — printable admin view at `/admin/kitchen-list` (two columns: "Finalized at 8 PM" + "Late changes"). The dedicated real-time kitchen display (option a in the design discussion) is a follow-up only if the printable view is insufficient after a week of use. (12) **No migration** — production has zero bookings (per decision 1); the legacy `hasBreakfast: boolean`, `breakfastRate: number`, `breakfastIncludesChildren: boolean`, `ratePerPersonPerNight`, `breakfastIncludesChildrenDefault` fields are deleted from the create path + Settings save payload + type definitions in the same PR that introduces the new shape. The read paths default the new fields to `{}` / `0` / `false` for any legacy test data. (13) **Audit subcollection, not a field on the booking doc.** `bookings/{id}/breakfastAudit/{auditId}` for every add/change/remove/serve/unserve/override action. Avoids the GOTCHAS "fan-out writes to a single array field" race. (14) **No new Vercel function.** The 6 new routes (1 public config read, 1 guest manage read, 1 guest update, 1 staff update, 1 mark-served, 1 kitchen list) are all handlers in the existing catch-all. (15) **No new email template.** The existing 8 W4.4 audit-extension templates + the `buildReservationEmailView` helper extend to render the new `breakfastSelections` shape. **Tickets proposed:** BR-01..BR-08 (data model, Settings, Step 2 + Step 1 cleanup, guest manage page, staff drawer, refunds, kitchen list, email/PDF/Reports). Realistic solo-dev timeline: 6–8 weeks. **Gates:** GOTCHAS §Firebase (no fan-out writes to a single array field — the audit is a subcollection; no client-side recompute of `breakfastTotal`), CRL-01 (refund idempotency reuses the existing `OnsitePayment` table + the existing `handleAddRefund` pattern), PEX-01..06 (the 24h post-Confirm hold + the in-flow hold + the new 8 PM breakfast cut-off are three independent lifecycles — none intersect), MRB-01 (the per-guest `guestIndex` is over the **reservation's aggregate guest list**, not per-room; the kitchen list prints one row per guest, not per room), VERCEL-FUNCTION-LIMIT (the catch-all dispatcher handles all new routes; the 12-function cap is unchanged), SECURITY.md (the public manage endpoint is gated on the `ref + lookupToken` magic-link credential; staff routes are staff-Token gated; the kitchen list read is staff-Token gated; Turnstile is enforced on every mutating call). **MD sync:** `BREAKFAST.md` (NEW spec), `SETTINGS.md §Breakfast & Dining` (extension), `BOOKING-FLOW.md` (Step 1 simplification + Step 2 new section note), `BOOKINGS-MANAGEMENT.md` (drawer rewrite note), `EMAIL-PDF-STORAGE.md` (breakfast-shape change in the view), `BACKEND.md` (new `breakfastConfig` shape + `bookings/*/breakfastAudit/*` rules + `bookings/*/refunds/*` rules), `TYPES.md` (new `BreakfastConfig` / `BreakfastMeal` / `BreakfastSelectionEntry` / `BreakfastServedEntry` / `BreakfastAuditEntry`), `ROADMAP.md` (Phase 13 row). **Rejected:** **(a)** keep the flat-rate model (cannot support per-meal pricing, manageability, or refund liability); **(b)** auto-pay for breakfast online at order time (inconsistent with how rooms work; refund path gets ugly; walk-in + staff-override split the model); **(c)** require a "No meal" placeholder per slot (forces the guest to fill every slot; the spec's "leave the slot empty" approach is simpler + naturally supports partial-stay breakfast); **(d)** build a dedicated real-time kitchen display in v1 (premature; ship printable admin view first); **(e)** migrate the existing `hasBreakfast: boolean` / `breakfastRate: number` fields (production is empty per decision 1; a parallel-models-forever alternative was deemed uglier than a clean delete); **(f)** add a separate `breakfastRefunds` collection (the existing `OnsitePayment` table with a new `category` field is the simpler path). |

## Active & Recent Decisions (#108 – #238)

| # | Decision |
|---|---|
| 108 | **Booking payment methods are a fully dynamic, admin-managed list** (Settings → Payment Methods). Structured `accountName` + `accountNumber` fields and `isEnabled` toggle replace legacy `accountInfo`. QR images stored at `assets/payment-methods/{method}/{filename}` in Firebase Storage. |
| 109 | **Protected payment methods:** `pay-at-hotel` and `add-to-bill` cannot be deleted from Settings or via direct API calls (`PROTECTED_PAYMENT_METHODS`). |
| 110 | **In-room store payment methods unified:** `settings/hotelConfig.paymentMethods[]` is the single source for booking, store, and corporate payment visibility. |
| 111 | **Independent surface visibility switches:** each payment method owns `isEnabled` (booking), `showInStore` (store), and `showInCorporate` (corporate). |
| 112 | **Google Sign-In is authentication only;** Spark Rewards enrollment requires explicit consent via Privacy Policy / Terms checkbox. |
| 113 | **Homepage featured room cards do not expose live per-room operational status.** Cards are type-driven marketing cards; operational status is not rendered on homepage cards. |
| 114 | **Self-service cross-provider auth linking is deferred to Phase 2.** Provider conflict message directs guests to sign in with their existing method first. |
| 115 | **BIR/VAT scope decision:** tax breakdown (12% VAT, VATable Sales, VAT-Exempt Sales, VAT Amount) calculated client-side for reports & XLSX exports. |
| 116 | **Direct-paid store orders join the shared collections ledger on delivery.** COD recorded as Cash; Add to Bill remains unsettled booking-folio charge. |
| 117 | **Early departure retains the contracted booking total.** Operational checkout shortens dates without auto-reducing total price. |
| 118 | **Spark Rewards points require a fully settled folio** and cover net booking spend only. |
| 119 | **Production environment split:** production database starts clean for bookings and transactions; active staff accounts pre-provisioned. |
| 120 | **Notification Center (Phase 12):** persisted `notifications` collection for admin alerts with 30-day retention cron. |
| 121 | **Purpose of stay is required at physical check-in and defaults to `Leisure`.** Add to admin drawer & guest registration form. |
| 122 | **Confirm with balance (CWB-01..05):** allows staff to transition a `payment-uploaded` booking to `confirmed` with an intentional partial payment. Reuses `unpaidCheckoutApprovalThreshold` (default 5,000) as four-eyes gate. |
| 123 | **Multi-booking picker for `/my-booking`:** when email-alone search matches >1 booking, returns a privacy-preserving list of 10 most recent stays. |
| 124 | **Unify payment reference number on payment ledger:** retired top-level `paymentReferenceNumber` in favor of per-payment `transactionReference`. |
| 125 | **HEIC client-side conversion:** use `heic-to@1.5.2` (LGPL-3.0) via dynamic `import()` for HEIC photo conversions in admin guest ID upload. |
| 126 | **Multi-booking picker privacy tightening:** uniform row shape with `maskedEmail` (`j***@gmail.com`), omitting `guestName`. |
| 127 | **Walk-in first/last name split:** modal collects first and last name separately, dropping string split. |
| 128 | **Drop `guestName` from email-alone 1-match single card:** public `/my-booking` does not reflect guest name back for email-alone queries. |
| 129 | **Turnstile token reuse for picker clicks:** prevents "Bot verification token missing" error during multi-booking navigation. |
| 130 | **Search form stays always-mounted:** suppresses with `hidden` / `aria-hidden` rather than unmounting, keeping Turnstile widget alive. |
| 131 | **Masked email on single-booking lookup card:** public `/my-booking` never exposes full email or guest name on any lookup path. |
| 132 | **Breakfast served map hydration:** `AdminContext` snapshot mapper hydates `breakfastServed` to persist "Mark Served" toggles across refreshes. |
| 135 | **Spark Rewards MED-3 (build variant): the front-desk manual "Link Existing Booking" surface (2026-07-29):** the smaller of MED-3's two paths — staff link an existing booking to a member by hand. New admin-only server endpoint `POST /api/members/link-booking` (`handleLinkBookingToMember`, `guest-app/server/handlers/members.ts`): a front-desk call is 403, the body is Zod-validated (`{ memberUid, bookingId, reason (1..500) }`), and it is rate-limited to 10/min/IP (mirroring `manual-adjust` + `set-active`). The server transaction re-reads the member AND the booking and refuses a cancelled booking (400), a test-run booking (400) and a booking already linked to a DIFFERENT member (409 — no silent overwrite and no unlink from this surface), then writes `memberId` + `linkedByStaff` + `linkedAt` + `linkedReason` on the booking plus an audit row under `bookings/audit/records/{bookingId}-link-{timestamp}` (the erasure-audit shape from #49). Re-linking the same booking is idempotent (no booking write; the audit row is still written and the response carries `alreadyLinked: true`). The admin member drawer gains a "Link Existing Booking" form with the member's email in the helper copy, so staff can confirm the different-email context before linking. **The guest self-service half of MED-3 is a separate, still-deferred decision (#140).** |
| 136 | **Spark Rewards LOW-3: `linkBookingsByEmail` is NOT chunked to Firestore's 500-write limit — DEFERRED (2026-07-25):** recorded as a decision to defer, not to fix. Theoretical only — it needs a member whose email matches more than 500 bookings. |
| 137 | **Editable Terms & Conditions & consent versioning:** terms body editable from Settings; booking consent captures version string. |
| 138 | **Guest store search & category browsing:** intercom store panel includes live search bar and category filters. |
| 139 | **House Rules in transactional emails:** embedded in payment-confirmed, booking-confirmed, and check-in reminder templates. |
| 140 | **Spark Rewards MED-3, guest self-service half: DEFERRED:** the post-sign-in prompt ("We found bookings under a different email. Would you like to link them?") with the booking email pre-filled was never built — none of the guest surfaces (`RewardsLandingPage`, `ProfilePage`, the post-booking confirmation page) offer a different-email reconciliation flow. Workaround: the guest looks the booking up at `/my-booking` (ref + email), and staff link it from the member detail drawer (#135). This is the larger of MED-3's two surfaces and is a separate build. |
| 141 | **Walk-in Payment Method from Settings (WPM):** walk-in modal payment selector dynamically loads from `settings/hotelConfig.paymentMethods[]`. |
| 142 | **New Booking & Customizable Booking Sources (NBS):** renamed modal to "New Booking", widened source to string, admin-editable booking sources in Settings. |
| 144 | **Child Capacity & Occupancy Rules (CHD):** max 2 children, adult and child capacity validated independently, extra beds apply to adult or child overflow. |
| 145 | **Extra Bed Pricing & Capacity (EXB):** per-room-type `maxExtraBeds` and `extraBedRate`, snapshotted on booking, extra bed total included in folio. |
| 146 | **Discount Scope Configuration (DSC):** admin-toggleable scope (room / breakfast / extra bed) per discount class (Senior/PWD, Voucher, Member). |
| 147 | **Pending Booking Expiry & Hold Window (PEX):** 24h default payment hold window, `holdExpiresAt` timestamp snapshot, daily cleanup cron (`/api/holds/expire`). |
| 148 | **DSC Scope Snapshots & Senior Guardrails:** `discountScopeSnapshot` persisted on booking; senior/PWD scope restricted to admin with statutory warning. |
| 149 | **VAT Breakdown Calculation (DSC-06):** 12% VAT, VATable Sales, VAT-Exempt Sales (Senior/PWD), and VAT Amount calculated client-side for reports & XLSX exports. |
| 150 | **VAT Breakdown Surfaces (DSC-07):** receipt PDF, admin drawer, and guest confirmation preview display 12% VAT reconciliation. |
| 151 | **Weekend Rate Visibility (WRV):** Step 1 room cards and Step 3 breakdown display non-regular nightly rate lines whenever non-regular rates apply. |
| 153 | **The server's `requiredExtraBedsFor` helper is the single source for how many extra beds an occupancy split needs (EXB, 2026-08-01):** the admin walk-in grid and the server derive the extra-bed requirement from the SAME helper, so the desk's preview and the server's validation can never disagree about how many extra beds a guest's adult/child split requires. |
| 154 | **The admin rates matrix gains a 4th column for the extra-bed price (EXB-05, 2026-08-01):** and it does not render a live rate before the settings snapshot arrives — no flash of a stale or undefined value on first paint. |
| 155 | **The occupancy pickers read the room type's stored capacity caps (EXB-07, 2026-08-01):** the guest booking form, the corporate booking form and the admin drawer all read the SAME stored per-room-type adult/child caps, so no surface can offer a split the inventory cannot hold. |
| 156 | **The receipt PDF and the booking-drawer workspace render the extra-bed line (EXB-08, 2026-08-01):** the receipt's detail rows carry the extra-bed occupants, so the printed receipt and the drawer agree about the same booking. |
| 157 | **The extra-bed INVENTORY is capped server-side by a shared helper (EXB-10, 2026-08-01):** `shared/utils/extraBedInventory.ts` owns the cap and the reschedule + add-room paths apply the same cap as the create path — otherwise a reschedule or an added room could double-book the hotel's extra-bed pool. |
| 158 | **The extra-bed rate is snapshotted on the booking, and the breakfast coupling is enforced (EXB-09, 2026-08-01):** the extra-bed price is frozen at order time like the room rate, so a later rate change cannot rewrite a booked booking's price; the breakfast coupling (extra-bed occupants are ordinary meal slots, not a separate flat rate) is enforced at the same time. |
| 159 | **Multi-Room Bookings & Reservation Header (MRB):** reservation header collection `reservations/{id}` with `reservationRef` (`R-YYYYMMDD-NNNNN`), linking child bookings. |
| 164 | **Reservation Header Idempotency & Write Operations:** transactional pre-allocated `reservationId` UUIDv4 + `requestFingerprint` SHA-256 for create, walk-in, reschedule, and corporate flows. |
| 165 | **Structured, snapshotted cancellation policy (CRL-05):** cancellation policy fields (`cancellationCutoffHours`, `cancellationRefundPctBefore`, `cancellationRefundPctAfter`) added as runtime-editable numbers in Legal Content settings. Reservations snapshot the policy fields and source at creation time. Evaluator handles cutoff rules independent of DST/local offsets, with legacy null snapshots falling back to standard 48-hour/100% rules and `"legacy-fallback"` source. |

One reservation-scope `booking-cancelled` email lists every cancelled room + aggregate state (MRB-09 expands the template inventory). One `sendBookingTrigger("booking-cancelled-reservation", view)` fires after the transaction commits (the MRB-09 multi-room template splits rooms into "cancelled" + "surviving" via the per-child `cancelledAt` stamp); the per-child path keeps the legacy `sendBookingTrigger("booking-cancelled", view)` call (which MRB-09 already taught to render the full reservation view when the booking has a `reservationId`). The first-created child has no special financial consequence — `reservations/{id}/payments` and `refunds/` are the source of truth. The `ConfirmForm` component gains an `additionalFields?: ReactNode` slot for the segmented control so other callers (order cancel, discount reject) keep the legacy reason-only shape. The guest `/my-booking` page (per MRB-10) already routes the cancel submit through `scope: "reservation"` when `activeReservation` is set; this PR makes the server honour the flag. `guest-app/tests/api/mrb-13-cancel-scope.test.ts` (29 source-text tests). | 171 | **CRL-06 Secure Cancellation Preview (proposed 2026-08-02):** `POST /api/bookings/cancel-preview` (URL is `cancel-preview` — hyphen-separated, same shape as `add-payment` / `create-walkin` / `confirm-with-balance` because the apiRouter splits on `/` and takes `[domain, action]`) is a read-only sibling of the destructive cancel. The guest body validates through `guestCancelPreviewSchema` (sibling of `guestCancelSchema` — same `ref + (email | token)` credential, optional `scope: "room" \| "reservation"` defaulting to `"room"`, no `reason` field since the preview is non-mutating); the staff body reads `req.body.scope` directly (no schema gate). The helper (`shared/utils/cancellation.ts`) takes the looked-up booking + reservation + cancellable children + net-collected total + `now` and returns the per-scope breakdown: per-room subtotal + per-room net-collected (pro-rated by the cancellable subtotal share — `paymentsTotal × (roomSubtotal / cancellableSubtotal)`, exact for the legacy single-booking case) + per-room `refundPct` (from each room's own `cancellationPolicySnapshot` via the existing `evaluateCancellation` helper — a corporate override per room is reflected in the per-room projection) + per-room `policyRefund` + per-room `retainedAmount`. The **aggregate** `refundPct` is the MINIMUM per-room `refundPct` — the worst-case floor the staff can guarantee without an exception (a higher per-room refund is visible in the per-room projection so staff can see the upside). The `staffProcessingRequired` flag is true when the policy refunds money AND the guest has paid — the destructive cancel never auto-refunds per CRL-04, so the staff must record a refund (CRL-07's admin workflow) after the cancel commits. The handler returns the new `CancellationPreview` shape (`shared/types/index.ts`): `kind: "single" \| "reservation"`, per-scope `room` / `rooms[]` projections, aggregate `subtotal` / `netCollected` / `policyRefund` / `retainedAmount` / `staffProcessingRequired`, shared `cutoffHours` / `cutoffTimeMs` / `hoursRemaining` / `isBeforeCutoff` / `refundPct` / `policyText` / `policySource`. The guest + admin modals render a `<CancellationPreviewPanel>` (one per app — admin is the canonical reusable, guest is a wider-card mirror) inside the `ConfirmForm`'s `additionalFields` slot. The panel surfaces the loading / error / success states (`data-testid="cancellation-preview-panel"` for the success render, `-loading` for the spinner, `-error` for the "breakdown unavailable" copy) and re-fetches on scope flip (admin) or modal open (guest). Gates: CRL-07 (the admin workflow + the policy-refund exception flow consume the preview's `staffProcessingRequired` flag), CRL-08 (the refund-state email consumes the preview's `refundPct` + `retainedAmount` + `policyText`), CRL-09 (the behavioural tests + the staging rehearsal). |
| 172 | **CRL-06 implementation correction (shipped 2026-08-02):** policy refund and retained amount are calculated from net collected, not the room subtotal, because unpaid balance cannot be refunded or retained. A room-scope preview on a multi-room reservation reads the reservation folio and allocates it using every currently cancellable sibling as the denominator; it never reads an empty legacy child ledger or attributes the whole reservation payment to one room. The guest preview now honours reservation scope from its validated body, and guest self-service expands to all four pre-arrival statuses only after the modal gained the financial preview. The post-confirmation lookup card immediately shows cancellation plus the preview-derived refund-processing state; persisted liability and processed-refund state remain CRL-07. Canonical response types are imported from shared code by both apps, and the calculation contract is covered by behavioral unit tests in addition to the source guards.
173 | **CRL-07 Reservation Refund Liability + Admin Workflow (proposed 2026-08-03):** the destructive cancel (`handleCancelBooking`) now **materializes** the policy result onto the cancelled entity in the same transaction as the status flip, so the financial snapshot is durable and re-derivable. The new field `cancellationLiability` lives on the reservation header (`reservations/{id}.cancellationLiability`) for reservation-scope cancels and new-path N=1 cancels, and on the booking doc (`bookings/{id}.cancellationLiability`) for legacy null-`reservationId` bookings and per-child cancels inside a multi-room reservation. The shape is `{ policyResult: { refundPct, policyRefund, netCollected, retainedAmount, cutoffHours, source, snapshottedAt }, approvedAmount, exception: { approvedAmount, reason, approvedBy, approvedAt } \| null }`. **`policyResult` is immutable** — a later settings change never rewrites a cancelled booking's snapshot; the server reads `policyResult` for display, never recomputes. **`approvedAmount` defaults to `policyResult.policyRefund`** and is reduced only via a new admin-only `POST /api/bookings/cancellation-exception` endpoint that requires an admin UID and a non-empty reason (capped at 500 chars); the endpoint appends an `exception` audit object and updates `approvedAmount` atomically. The destructive cancel itself never auto-refunds (CRL-04), so the admin records processed refunds over time using the existing admin-only `POST /api/bookings/add-refund` (the writer writes to `reservations/{id}/refunds/{refundId}` for new reservations and `bookings/{id}/payments/{refundId}` for legacy — the dual-write path MRB-04 Phase 2.x shipped). A pure `computeCancellationLiabilityState` helper in `shared/utils/cancellation.ts` takes the stored liability + the current cumulative `processedAmount` (derived from the refunds subcollection) and returns one of five states: `not-required` (`policyRefund === 0` — nothing to refund), `retained` (`approvedAmount < policyRefund` — admin applied an exception; the retention amount `policyRefund - approvedAmount` is the "extra we kept"), `pending-processing` (`approvedAmount === policyRefund` AND `processedAmount === 0`), `partially-processed` (`0 < processedAmount < approvedAmount`), `processed` (`processedAmount >= approvedAmount`). Derived fields (state + outstanding = approved − processed) are NEVER stored — the UI + Reports derive them from the ledger. Front desk (any staff role) can cancel; **only admins** can record a processed refund or apply an exception (mirrors the existing `handleAddRefund` admin-only gate, no new authorization surface). Reservation-scope cancel writes ONE liability snapshot to the reservation header (the aggregate, derived from the reservation-scope preview); per-child cancel writes ONE snapshot to the booking doc; legacy null-`reservationId` cancel writes the snapshot to the booking doc (byte-equivalent to per-child, the booking is its own reservation). The admin BookingsPage gains a `CancellationLiabilityPanel` rendered inside the cancelled-booking drawer's Folio section when `cancellationLiability` is present: it shows the policy row (immutable), the approved row (admin-controlled), the processed row (live from the refunds subcollection), the outstanding row (derived), the state badge, and two action buttons gated on `currentUser?.role === "admin"`. The "Record processed refund" button opens the existing refund modal pre-filled with the outstanding amount (the staff can still override) and reuses the idempotency contract from CRL-01. The "Apply exception" button opens a small modal with an amount input (must be `0 ≤ amount ≤ policyRefund`) and a reason input (required, ≤500 chars); on submit the page calls the new endpoint and the panel re-renders. Surviving rooms in a partial reservation-scope cancel do NOT carry a liability field (their status is unchanged); the reservation header is the source of truth for the aggregate. `processedAmount` is recomputed on every render by reading `reservations/{id}/refunds/` (or `bookings/{id}/payments/` for legacy) — Reports (CRL-08 follow-up) will use the same projection. N=1 byte-equivalent: a cancel whose snapshot is `policyRefund === 0` (e.g. past the cutoff + no payment) skips the snapshot field entirely (the absence means "no liability work to do") so the legacy booking list + Reports continue to read from the booking doc without knowing CRL-07 exists. Source-text guard + state-helper behavioral tests: `guest-app/tests/api/crl-07-refund-liability.test.ts`. Rejected: a `cancellationLiabilities/` top-level subcollection (denormalized writes + the docs would need their own `bookingId`/`reservationId` linkage — adding a field to the cancelled entity is simpler and the `where` query in Reports already filters by status); storing `processedAmount` as a denormalized field (the spec says "derived from immutable ledger entries" — Reports must read the ledger, not a possibly-stale counter); a separate `exceptionHistory[]` array (the `exception` field is a single object overwritten on each new exception, the audit row lives in the admin notifications collection CRL-08 adds); auto-recording a partial refund at cancel time (CRL-04's "never auto-refund" rule); staff roles other than admin recording a processed refund (the spec explicitly gates that on admin — a front-desk operator who collects the GCash refund still needs an admin to "Record processed refund"); rewriting the policy result on a settings change (the snapshot is the contract — `policyResult` is read-only post-cancel). Gates: CRL-06 (the preview's `staffProcessingRequired` flag drives the new panel's render gate), CRL-08 (the refund-state email + Reports liability queue consume the new state), CRL-09 (the behavioural tests + staging rehearsal).
174 | **CRL-08 Refund-state emails, persistent staff notifications, and Reports liability queue (proposed 2026-08-03):** the destructive cancel (CRL-07) and the admin's subsequent processed refunds now generate a coherent email + notification + Reports trail, so the guest knows the money story and the staff have a single place to monitor pending liability. **(1) Cancellation email now lists the financial breakdown** — both `booking-cancelled` and `booking-cancelled-reservation` templates render the reservation's policy refund, retained amount, revised total/balance, and the current processing state (`No refund owed` / `Pending refund` / etc.) using the CRL-07 `cancellationLiability` snapshot the destructive cancel stamped. The `loadReservationEmailView` helper reads the stored snapshot (reservation header for reservation-scope cancels + new-path N=1, booking doc for per-child + legacy null-`reservationId` cancels), runs `computeCancellationLiabilityState` with the current `processedAmount` (a fresh read so the email reflects the live state at the moment of send, not the cancel-time state), and attaches a `{ liability: { policyResult, approvedAmount, processedAmount, outstandingAmount, retentionAmount, state, stateLabel } }` projection. The template's "no refund is issued automatically" callout now references the actual numbers (e.g. "₱1,500 of your ₱5,000 paid will be refunded; ₱3,500 is retained per the policy"). **(2) New `booking-refund-processed` email action** — fires from `handleAddRefund` when a successful refund commit changes the liability state (the prior state and the new state are both computed via `computeCancellationLiabilityState` with the same `processedAmount` read; a state change is the trigger, so an idempotent replay or a sub-100-peso partial within the same state does NOT re-send). The email lists the new state, the new processed total, the new outstanding amount, the retention (if any), the payment method, and the transaction reference. Notifications are best-effort (the existing `writeNotification` helper, the "never fail the caller" pattern from #120). The queries iterate `reservations` + `bookings` with `cancellationLiability != null` (the dual-collection read because the snapshot lives on whichever entity the cancel stamp chose — reservation header OR booking doc), sum the refunds subcollection per liability for the live `processedAmount`, and surface the same five-state machine. Exports + Daily Close continue to derive actual cash movement from the payment ledger, never from `approvedAmount` (per #173's "derived from immutable ledger entries" rule) — the liability queue is a separate surface that surfaces the lifecycle state, not a separate money book. Source-text guard: `guest-app/tests/api/crl-08-refund-state-emails-and-reports.test.ts`. Rejected: a denormalized `cancellationLiabilities/` collection (the spec body says "derived from immutable ledger entries" — Reports must read the ledger); re-sending the email on every refund entry (the state-change gate is the right trigger, otherwise the guest gets 5 emails for 5 partials in the same state); a separate "exception-applied" notification (the exception's audit row on the `cancellationLiability.exception` field is the persistent trail — the staff sees it on the panel + the bell notification carries the state change, no separate alert needed); auto-recording the refund on the cancel transaction (CRL-04's "never auto-refund" rule, unchanged); firing the email on an idempotent replay (the `idempotentReplay: true` branch returns the existing record without changing state, so no email fires). Three new endpoints ship on the same branch: (1) `POST /api/bookings/cancellation-exception` (admin-only, requires `reservationId` OR `bookingId` + `approvedAmount: 0..policyRefund` + non-empty `reason: ≤500 chars`; idempotency on `(approvedAmount, reason)`; reduces `approvedAmount` + overwrites the `exception` audit row); (2) `POST /api/bookings/cancellation-liability` (any authenticated staff; reads the stored liability + the cumulative refunds subcollection and returns the live `CancellationLiabilityStateOutput` via the pure `computeCancellationLiabilityState` helper); (3) the existing admin-only `POST /api/bookings/add-refund` (CRL-01) continues to be the writer for processed refunds (the dual-write path the existing handler uses — `reservations/{id}/refunds/{refundId}` for new reservations, `bookings/{id}/payments/{refundId}` for legacy — stays byte-equivalent; the new fields on the refund record are `reservationId` + `bookingId` for per-room attribution). The admin BookingsPage gains a `CancellationLiabilityPanel` (in `admin-app/src/components/CancellationLiabilityPanel.tsx`) mounted in the cancelled-booking drawer's Folio section when `cancellationLiability` is present: shows the policy row (immutable) + the approved row (admin-controlled) + the processed row (live from the refunds subcollection) + the outstanding row (derived) + the state badge + the retention callout (when an exception was applied) + two action buttons gated on `currentUser?.role === "admin"`. The "Record processed refund" button opens the existing refund modal pre-filled with the outstanding amount (the staff can still override); the "Apply exception" button opens a `CancellationExceptionModal` with an amount input (must be `0 ≤ amount ≤ policyRefund`) + a required reason input (≤500 chars). The `CancellationLiabilityPanel` re-projects on every render via a `refreshKey` prop the parent bumps after a successful refund or exception (so the panel re-fetches the live `processedAmount` without waiting for the Firestore onSnapshot to land). N=1 byte-equivalent: a cancel whose snapshot is `policyRefund === 0` skips the field entirely so the legacy booking list + Reports continue to read from the booking doc without knowing CRL-07 exists. The two new helpers live in `shared/utils/cancellation.ts` next to CRL-05/CRL-06's `evaluateCancellation` / `evaluateCancelPreview`; the projection endpoint defers the import to avoid a circular dependency at module load time. The admin `Booking` type hydrates `cancellationLiability` from the Firestore snapshot in `AdminContext.tsx` (the field reads as `null` when the booking has no liability — pre-CRL-07 cancels, no-refund cancels, or active bookings). Source-text guard + state-helper behavioural tests: `guest-app/tests/api/crl-07-refund-liability.test.ts` (24 source-text tests) + `shared/__tests__/crl-07-liability-state.test.ts` (23 behavioural tests covering each state, the null fall-through, defensive coercion, the breakdown invariants, and the snapshot builder). Rejected (re-confirmed from #173): a denormalized `cancellationLiabilities/` collection (the field-on-entity is simpler); a separate `exceptionHistory[]` array (the `exception` field is a single object overwritten on each new exception — the audit row lives in the admin notifications collection CRL-08 adds); a separate `liabilityState` denormalized field (the helper computes it on demand — Reports + the panel re-derive on every render). Gates: CRL-06 (the preview's `staffProcessingRequired` flag drives the new panel's render gate), CRL-08 (the refund-state email + Reports liability queue consume the new state), CRL-09 (the end-to-end behavioural tests + staging rehearsal).
176 | **CRL-08 implementation record (shipped 2026-08-03):** the destructive cancel + each successful admin refund now generate a coherent email + notification + Reports trail. **(1) Cancellation email** — both `booking-cancelled` (per-child + legacy null-`reservationId` cancels) and `booking-cancelled-reservation` (reservation-scope cancels per MRB-13) templates read the CRL-07 `liabilityProjection` field via the new `loadLiabilityProjectionForEmail` helper in `guest-app/server/handlers/email.ts`. The helper reads the stored snapshot (reservation header for new reservations, booking doc for legacy) + the cumulative `processedAmount` from the refunds subcollection (the dual-source read MRB-04 Phase 2.x uses) and runs `computeCancellationLiabilityState` to project the live state. The `loadReservationEmailView` helper (which both cancel paths call) attaches the projection to the view so the templates can read it. The legacy null-`reservationId` cancel path in `handleCancelBooking` also loads the projection for the bare-booking view (`reservationView ? reservationView.liabilityProjection : await loadLiabilityProjectionForEmail({ bookingId })`). A shared `liabilityBreakdownCard(projection)` helper renders a six-row table (net collected at cancel, policy refund, retained under policy, approved, processed so far, outstanding, current state) — the per-child + reservation-scope paths render the same card so the two templates never drift. The "what happens next" callout tailors itself to the actual numbers when the projection is present (e.g. "₱1,500 of your ₱5,000 paid will be refunded; ₱3,500 is retained per the policy"); the legacy generic copy stays for pre-CRL-07 cancels + the no-refund case. **(2) New `booking-refund-processed` email action** — fires from `handleAddRefund` when a successful commit changes the liability state. The state-change gate lives inside the same `runTransaction` as the refund write: the handler reads the stored liability + the current `processedAmount` (BEFORE this refund) to compute the prior state, then re-computes the new state with `processedAmount = prior + numericAmount`. The closure-scoped locals `priorStateRef` + `newStateRef` carry the values to the post-commit side-effect. A state change is the trigger; an idempotent replay (the existing CRL-01 contract) or a sub-state partial does NOT re-send. The post-commit helper `fireRefundStateEmailAndNotification` fires the email + writes the staff notification. The email template (`bookingRefundProcessedEmail`) tailors the headline + "what this means" callout to the new state — `processed` / `partially-processed` / `pending-processing` / `retained` — and lists the latest refund's method + reference in a dedicated card. The reservation-scope + per-child + legacy paths all route through the same template (the `loadLiabilityProjectionForEmail` helper reads the appropriate subcollection). **(3) Persistent staff notifications** — adds a new `cancellation-refund` value to the `NotificationType` union in `shared/types/index.ts`. `handleCancelBooking` writes one after the destructive cancel commits, when the just-stamped `cancellationLiability` is non-null (the desk sees a "money to process" alert with the policy refund + the new state in the title). `handleAddRefund` writes one when a successful commit changes the state — the title is "Refund <state> — <ref> (from → to)" so the lifecycle progression is visible at a glance. Both writes are best-effort (the existing `writeNotification` helper, the "never fail the caller" pattern from #120). The `NotificationBell` gains a new `cancellation-refund` icon (Wallet, amber palette) in `NOTIFICATION_TYPE_META` — no other client changes for the bell surface. **(4) Reports "Liability" tab** — new tab in `admin-app/src/pages/ReportsPage.tsx` (sibling to Performance / Sales / Daily Close) mounts the new `LiabilityTab` component (`admin-app/src/components/LiabilityTab.tsx`). The tab self-fetches every `cancellationLiability` snapshot via the dual-source read (collectionGroup on `reservations` for new reservations + per-booking read for legacy) + sums the refunds subcollection per liability for the live `processedAmount` (a `collectionGroup` on `refunds` is the batched-optimisation path if/when the property grows — the spec explicitly defers the "bound the listeners" follow-up to FLR-03's 1-year-of-operation trigger). Headline metrics: pending count (sum of `pending-processing` + `partially-processed`), pending amount (sum of `outstandingAmount`), partials (count of `partially-processed`), processed total in range (from `cancelledAt` in the report window), retained cancellation revenue in range (when `retentionAmount > 0`). Age distribution bucketed into under-7d / 7-30d / over-30d for the pending items (the >30d red-flag is the desk's primary chase target). The pending list is sorted oldest-first; the full liability list shows every snapshot for audit. Exports + Daily Close continue to derive actual cash movement from the payment ledger, never from `approvedAmount` (per #173's "derived from immutable ledger entries" rule) — the liability queue is a separate surface that surfaces the lifecycle state. **Source-text guard:** `guest-app/tests/api/crl-08-refund-state-emails-and-reports.test.ts` (19 tests covering the notification type, the breakdown-aware templates, the `loadLiabilityProjectionForEmail` helper, the `booking-refund-processed` action + template, the state-change gate in `handleAddRefund`, the staff notification in `handleCancelBooking`, the Reports `LiabilityTab`, and the `NotificationBell` meta). Rejected (re-confirmed from #174): a denormalized `cancellationLiabilities/` collection (the spec body says "derived from immutable ledger entries" — Reports must read the ledger); re-sending the email on every refund entry (the state-change gate is the right trigger, otherwise the guest gets 5 emails for 5 partials in the same state); a separate "exception-applied" notification (the exception's audit row on the `cancellationLiability.exception` field is the persistent trail — the staff sees it on the panel + the bell notification carries the state change, no separate alert needed); auto-recording the refund on the cancel transaction (CRL-04's "never auto-refund" rule, unchanged); firing the email on an idempotent replay (the `idempotentReplay: true` branch returns the existing record without changing state, so no email fires). Gates: CRL-07 (the stored liability + the state machine + the admin-only refund path are the inputs), CRL-09 (the behavioural end-to-end rehearsal — partial coverage shipped here, full CRL-09 cycle closes the loop).

For legacy null-`reservationId` bookings the booking doc itself is the source of truth — it counts as 1 reservation + 1 room stay in the header metrics. **(2) Child-line metrics (read from `bookings/{id}`)** — occupancy (room-nights sold / room-nights available), rooms sold (count of `checked-in` or later statuses), room-nights (sum of `getOverlapNights(checkIn, checkOut, periodStart, periodEnd)` per child), room cancellations (count of children with `status: "cancelled"`), **allocated room revenue** (the new `Booking.revenueAllocation.roomNet` per child), **allocated breakfast revenue** (the new `Booking.revenueAllocation.breakfastNet` per child), ADR (room revenue / rooms sold), RevPAR (room revenue / available room-nights), guest + adult + child occupancy splits, rate-per-night histograms. **(3) The stored-allocation rule** — every booking created after MRB-11 lands carries a `revenueAllocation: { roomNet, breakfastNet, addOnNet, deductionNet, totalNet }` field snapshot at create time, computed by the existing `calculatePricingBreakdown` chain (room rate × nights → gross room; breakfast rate × guests × nights → gross breakfast; voucher / corporate / member / Senior / PWD → pro-rated deductions per the same per-stream chain as the room rate; everything rounds to 2dp; `totalNet === booking.totalPrice` by construction, asserted at the create-write boundary). the export rows tag the latter as `"allocation: legacy-heuristic"` so the accountant can tell estimated splits from exact ones. **(4) Surface changes** — `admin-app/src/pages/ReportsPage.tsx` (every KPI + chart + table that uses `splitBookingRevenue` switches to `getBookingRevenueStreams`; the Performance occupancy + Sales revenue + Daily Close cash-movement + Liability "retained cancellation revenue" all read from the new helper); `admin-app/src/components/LiabilityTab.tsx` (the "retained cancellation revenue in range" metric is already the sum of `cancellationLiability.retentionAmount` — unchanged, but the documentation now states this is per-snapshot, not per-booking-derived); The legacy fallback preserves the current `splitBookingRevenue` math exactly so the byte-equivalent single-day reports (no allocation field on any doc) match the current numbers — no surprise jumps in the day this ships. **(6) Tests** — `shared/__tests__/mrb-11-revenue-streams.test.ts` (pure helper: stored allocation sums to `totalPrice` exactly; the add-on + deduction per-stream handling matches `calculatePricingBreakdown`); `admin-app/src/__tests__/mrb-11-reports-owner.test.ts` (Reports unit tests: a 3-room reservation in a Sales range reports reservation count = 1, room count = 3, allocated room revenue = sum of children, allocated breakfast revenue = sum of children; cancelling one room does NOT change the revenue of the surviving two; the "legacy-heuristic" tag appears only on rows whose `revenueAllocation` is null; the Daily Close cash-movement total equals the sum of the reservation folios, not the sum of `b.totalPrice`); storing only `roomNet` and computing `breakfastNet = totalPrice - roomNet` (fragile when add-ons exist — the breakfast revenue stream collapses to whatever the discount left over); a separate `allocationHistory[]` array on each booking (over-engineered — the allocation is fixed at create time + on reschedule, not edited in-place); a denormalized `revenueAllocations/` collection (the field-on-entity is simpler and the existing `where("reservationId", "==", id)` query already iterates the children); reading the gross `rateBreakdown.roomSubtotal` and subtracting the discount as a single line (loses the per-stream split — the deduction attribution gets pushed into a black box); | 178 | **MRB-11 implementation record (shipped 2026-08-03):** the Reports surface now reads stored per-stream revenue allocations. The per-stream values are GROSS (room rate × nights, breakfast rate × guests × nights, extra-bed subtotal); `deductionNet` is the single line for the senior + voucher + member deductions. The invariant `roomNet + breakfastNet + addOnNet - deductionNet === totalNet` holds by construction. 24 new behavioural tests in `shared/__tests__/mrb-11-revenue-streams.test.ts` (stored read, legacy fallback byte-equivalence to `splitBookingRevenue`, defensive null/undefined coercion, the reservation aggregate path, the invariant assertion throw + tolerance + chaining, and the compute helper for room-only / room+breakfast / room+breakfast+addOn / full senior+voucher+member stack / 2dp rounding / nullish defensive coercion). **(2) PR2 — server-side snapshot in every create path (commit `f27978a`)** — wires the foundation helpers into `handleCreateBooking` (the public create path), `handleCreateWalkin`, and `handleRescheduleBooking`. Every booking created after this commit lands carries a stored `revenueAllocation` snapshot; every reservation header carries `aggregateRevenueAllocation`. The assertion site runs INSIDE the same `runTransaction` as the price write so a miscalculation throws at the boundary, not the export. Public create: the per-child allocation is computed in the per-child write loop (the per-child `roomTotal` + `breakfastTotal` + `extraBedTotal` are already there; `deductionNet` is the sum of the per-deduction allocated amounts for that child); the reservation header aggregate is the sum of children. Walkin: the per-line allocation is computed inside the `walkinRoomStayFinancials` map callback (walkin has no member step, so the deduction sum is `seniorDeduction + voucherDeduction` from the chain result); the reservation header aggregate sums the N per-line allocations. Reschedule: re-snapshots because the dates (and therefore the gross amounts) have changed; the deduction sum includes the chain's senior + voucher + member deductions AND the snapshotted `pointsRedeemedValue` (a separate deduction layer the chain does not see — `finalTotalPrice = totalPrice - pointsRedeemedValue`); the reservation header aggregate is set to the per-child allocation for the single-room reschedule case (the current scope of the handler). N>1 multi-child reschedule is a pre-existing limitation (the reservation `totalPrice` is also stale for a multi-child reschedule); Reports falls back to summing children via `getReservationRevenueStreams`, which is always correct. 9 new source-text guards in `guest-app/tests/api/mrb-11-revenue-allocation-snapshot.test.ts` (import + helper re-exports + per-child + aggregate write sites in all three handlers + at-least-6 `assertBookingRevenueAllocationInvariant` call sites in `bookings.ts` as the write-boundary guard). **(3) PR3 — Reports surface switch (commit `74be5ef`)** — every `splitBookingRevenue(b).room` / `.breakfast` call site in `ReportsPage.tsx` (6 sites) now reads `getBookingRevenueStreams(b).roomNet` / `.breakfastNet`. The per-stream values are now GROSS for post-MRB-11 docs (the deduction is a single line in the export). The pre-existing FL-01 test failure (409 vs 400) is unrelated to this item — confirmed pre-existing flake on stashed dev state. **MDs updated** — `plan/features/REPORTS.md` (summary-card checklist rewritten to describe the new per-stream contract + the Allocation column; manual-QA checklist pins the new invariant `room + breakfast + addOn - deduction === totalPrice`); `plan/docs/BACKEND.md` (new rows for `revenueAllocation` on the booking doc + `aggregateRevenueAllocation` on the reservation header with the invariant + the byte-equivalent fallback rule); `plan/project/ROADMAP.md` (MRB-11 flipped to ✅ shipped). **Test coverage** — 24 pure-helper tests (shared) + 9 source-text guards (guest-app/api) + 7 Reports unit tests (admin-app) = 40 new tests across 3 test files. **Follow-ups** (not blocking): the `firebase/tests/mrb-11-report-reconstruction.emulator.test.ts` round-trip test (Java + Firestore emulator not installed locally per the CRL-09 precedent; will ship when staging CI has Java); the top-of-export `"legacy-heuristic"` disclaimer banner (the spec's 5% line — a small follow-up PR adds the "Note: <N> bookings in this range pre-date MRB-11 and use the legacy proportional split" line at the top of the XLSX + PDF + CSV when any row is tagged legacy-heuristic). **Rejected (re-confirmed from #177)** — the proportional-by-stream-share net split (each `roomNet` is `roomTotal - roomNet × deductionNet / subtotal`); the separate `revenueAllocations/` collection; the `legacy-heuristic` flag field on the booking doc; the gross-only `addOnNet` without the `deductionNet` line (the two go together — the report view needs the gross per-stream to be honest about what each line generated, and the deduction line to be honest about what was given up). **Gates (re-confirmed from #177)** — MRB-09 (the reservation-scope email view already lists every room with its own per-room total — the Sales report can now do the same without an extra read), CRL-08 (the "retained cancellation revenue in range" metric on the new Liability tab finally has an exact backbone), MRB-13 (the cancel scope already updates the header counters), MRB-15 (the report-reconstruction property tests ship with this item — the emulator test is the Java-gated follow-up). |

| 179 | **MRB-12 Admin Reservation Embed (proposed 2026-08-03, decision reserved at spec time):** the Bookings table reservation row + the booking drawer's reservation strip + the discount form's scope selector are rewritten so the group-level state reads from the `Reservation` header instead of from the filtered in-memory children. **The driving bug** is at `admin-app/src/pages/BookingsPage.tsx:1701-1705` (pre-MRB-12): `groupedRows` summed the FILTERED children for `reservationTotal` (`child.totalPrice`) and `listReservationBalance` (`getBookingFolio(child).balance`), so a desk officer filtering by `brt=Deluxe` on a 3-room group with 1 Standard + 2 Deluxe saw the Standard room silently dropped from the math. The header doesn't filter — reading it instead of the children fixes the bug by construction. **(1) `AdminContext` listener wiring** — `subscribeToReservations` is a full-collection `onSnapshot` (no `orderBy`) that hydrates `Reservation[]` into the context. The listener is small (~14 active reservations at this hotel; the cap is bounded by room inventory, not bookings volume). A second listener is a `collectionGroup("payments")` filter-on-path-regex aggregate that sums positive-amount entries on `reservations/{id}/payments/{paymentId}` and stores `Record<reservationId, paidAmount>`. Legacy `bookings/{id}/payments/{paymentId}` entries are excluded by the regex anchor (`^reservations\/([^/]+)\/payments\/`) — they belong to the per-child legacy flow and stay summed from the in-memory `bookings` array. The sign-aware sum matches the `paymentsTotal` semantics `getReservationFolioSummary` exposes. **(2) `BookingListRow` shape** — the row builder attaches `listReservationHeader?: Reservation` + `listReservationPaidAmount?: number` to the synthesised reservation row so the Status column (MRB-12-02) reads the aggregate from the row, not a separate `useMemo`. Absent on N=1 + legacy null-`reservationId` rows (byte-equivalent). **(3) Status column (MRB-12-02)** — for `listRowKind === "reservation"` rows, the Status column renders a tone-mapped aggregate pill (`Awaiting` / `Verified` / `Confirmed` / `In-house` / `Completed` / `Cancelled`) reading `Reservation.paymentStatus`. The outstanding amount surfaces inline when the group is not yet settled. The legacy `Mixed` chip is the cold-start fallback only — the next snapshot replaces it with the aggregate pill. When `Reservation.cancelledRoomCount > 0`, an additional `X cancelled` chip renders with a tooltip listing the cancelled room numbers. **(4) Drawer reservation strip (MRB-12-03)** — the existing strip at `admin-app/src/pages/BookingsPage.tsx:4347-4378` (ref + position + sibling-room buttons) gains three pills (Total / Paid / Balance) reading the `Reservation` header + the reservation-scope paid-amount aggregate. The per-room `BookingDrawerWorkspaceHeader` below keeps its own per-room money; the strip's pills are the GROUP-level read, not a replacement. Hidden when `selectedReservationContext` is null (N=1 / legacy) — the strip already gates on that. **(5) Action-scope chip (MRB-12-04)** — the `Apply discount` button in the drawer's More section gains a `renderActionScope("room")` chip (the other multi-room actions — Confirm pay-at-hotel, Confirm booking, Move / upgrade room, Check in, Review proof in Folio, Collect balance, Confirm with Balance, Cancel — already have the chip from MRB-07 / MRB-13). N=1 + legacy paths keep their current action labels (no chip — no other room exists). **(6) Discount form segmented control (MRB-12-05)** — the modal gains a `This room` / `All N rooms` segmented control mirroring the MRB-13 cancel-modal pattern (`data-testid="staff-discount-scope-selector"`, `data-testid="staff-discount-scope-room"`, `data-testid="staff-discount-scope-reservation"`). `scope=room` calls the existing per-booking `apply-discount` endpoint (byte-equivalent to today). `scope=reservation` iterates `selectedReservationContext.rooms` and calls the same endpoint for each — errors on a single room abort the loop and surface the failure to the desk; already-repriced rooms keep their new totals; the operator retries the failed room. The modal title + submit button label + toast switch with the selected scope. The scope state resets to `null` on close (mirrors the MRB-13 `bookingCancelScope` reset). The API surface does not change. **(7) Tests** — 14 new source-text tests in `admin-app/src/__tests__/mrb-12-admin-reservation-affordance.test.ts`: (1) AdminContext hydrates `reservations` from a `reservations` collection listener; (2) AdminContext exposes `reservations` + `reservationPaidAmount` in the context value; (3) AdminContext aggregates the reservation-scope `paidAmount` from a `collectionGroup("payments")` listener with the path-anchored regex; (4) BookingsPage builds a `reservationsMap` lookup for the row builder; (5) row's `totalPrice` is read from the `Reservation` header, not the child sum; (6) row's `listReservationBalance` is `header.totalPrice − paidAmount`, not the child sum; (7) row builder depends on `reservationsMap` and `reservationPaidAmount`; (8) `BookingListRow` carries `listReservationHeader` + `listReservationPaidAmount`; (9) Status column renders the aggregate `paymentStatus` pill (not the legacy `Mixed` chip); (10) `X cancelled` chip renders when `cancelledRoomCount > 0`; (11) drawer reservation strip shows the reservation-scope Total / Paid / Balance pills; (12) `Apply discount` carries the `renderActionScope("room")` chip; (13) discount form has the segmented control mirroring MRB-13 + the loop over `selectedReservationContext.rooms`; (14) the scope state resets to `null` on close. The pre-existing MRB-07 test (`mrb-07-admin-multi-room.test.ts`) that asserted the old child-sum pattern is rewritten to pin the new header-sourced contract. **(8) MDs to update** — `plan/features/BOOKINGS-MANAGEMENT.md` (new "Multi-room reservation affordances" subsection + the "summary never requires N children" contract + the aggregate-status-pill + cancellation-count chip in the row's status column + the drawer strip's three pills + the discount-form scope selector); `plan/docs/BACKEND.md` (the `reservations/{id}` listener in AdminContext + the header-as-source-of-truth invariant for the row summary + the `collectionGroup("payments")` path-anchored aggregate); `plan/project/ROADMAP.md` (MRB-12 flipped to ✅ shipped); `plan/docs/DECISIONS-FEATURES.md #179` (this entry — implementation record). **(9) Rejected** — a new `GET /api/reservations/{id}/summary` endpoint (the AdminContext listener + the existing `getReservationFolioSummary` pure helper is the surface; no API change); a separate `ReservationsPage` (out of scope — the Bookings table row IS the affordance); rendering every child inline on the row (the existing Show/Hide rooms expander from #159 already handles that; the chip only adds a count badge); a per-stream revenue card on the row (MRB-11's stored `aggregateRevenueAllocation` is the source, the row reads the aggregate total — Reports owns the per-stream breakdown); a server-side `applyReservationDiscount` endpoint in MRB-12 (the loop is the same code path the desk would manually run today; adding a new endpoint for MRB-12 is speculative — ship the loop, add the endpoint when MRB-14 needs atomicity); a denormalized `paidAmount` field on the `Reservation` header (the `collectionGroup("payments")` aggregate is the source of truth — a denormalized field would drift on every refund + require transactional updates to all the refund write paths); a per-row `useMemo` keyed on the row's id (the row builder attaches the header in one pass; per-row lookups are N+1 over the same map). **(10) Gates** — MRB-14 (add-room + in-stay date changes — the segmented control pattern is the template for the "Add room to reservation" form; the `reservations/{id}` listener the add-room handler will need to update is the same listener MRB-12 ships); MRB-15 (the byte-equivalence for N=1 + legacy null-`reservationId` paths is the template for the cleanup pass; the report-reconstruction property tests are the Java-gated follow-up); BDUX (multiple verification rows #144-152 close automatically — the row's group-level read, the strip's money pills, and the action-scope chip on every multi-room action together satisfy the "no-scroll, all-status, no-hidden-rooms" criteria). |

| 180 | **MRB-14 Post-Create Room Changes (shipped 2026-08-03, implementation record):** staff can add a room to an existing pre-arrival reservation using the header's current dates; the per-child reschedule handler no longer mutates the header's `checkIn` / `checkOut`; per-child dates surface on every UI + email + receipt surface when the children diverge from the header. Shipped in 3 PRs on `feature/phase-12-mrb-14-post-create-room-changes` + `feature/phase-12-mrb-14-04-per-child-dates-ui` (v0.246.0 → v0.248.0). **(1) PR1 — `Reservation.actualDateRange` field + reschedule refactor (v0.246.0)** — new optional field `actualDateRange: { earliestCheckIn: Date, latestCheckOut: Date, isDivergent: boolean } \| null` on `Reservation` (`shared/types/index.ts`); the field is `null` for pre-MRB-14 reservations (no children to diverge, byte-equivalent to pre-MRB-14). New pure helper `computeReservationActualDateRange(children)` in `shared/utils/bookingFolio.ts` — derives the range by MIN(children.checkIn) / MAX(children.checkOut) and returns `null` for an empty children list (defensive — the handler should never call it with an empty list). The contract: `isDivergent === true` ⇔ ∃ a child whose `checkIn` or `checkOut` differs from the header's. The existing `Reservation.checkIn` / `checkOut` / `numNights` become immutable shared-dates snapshots from create time (server enforces; no CI test gate yet — the only mutator was the reschedule handler, which is the line removed below). New `AddRoomBookingSchema` in `shared/schemas/booking.ts` mirrors `RescheduleBookingSchema` + `WalkinRoomLineSchema`: `{ reservationId: RESERVATION_ID_REGEX, roomId, numAdults, numChildren, extraBedCount?, discountType?, voucherCode? }` — the dates are NEVER in the body. `handleCreateBooking` + `handleCreateWalkin` initialize `actualDateRange: { earliestCheckIn, latestCheckOut, isDivergent: false }` (every child has the same dates as the header on create, so `isDivergent` is always `false` at create time). `handleRescheduleBooking` no longer mutates the header's `checkIn` / `checkOut` / `numNights` — the existing `transaction.update(reservationDocRef, { checkIn, checkOut, numNights, ... })` line is removed; the handler now queries every child via `where("reservationId", "==", id)` in the same transaction + recomputes the header's `actualDateRange` (and re-stamps each child's `revenueAllocation` per the MRB-11 invariant). When all children still match the header's dates after the reschedule, `isDivergent` is set to `false`; when one child now extends past the header, `isDivergent` flips to `true` and the header tracks the new earliest / latest. `AdminContext` listener hydrates `actualDateRange` (defensive IIFE coerces `earliestCheckIn` / `latestCheckOut` via `parseDateOrNull` + returns `null` for missing or invalid values). 6 pure-helper tests in `shared/__tests__/mrb-14-actual-date-range.test.ts` (single-child range, multi-child MIN/MAX, empty children, `isDivergent: true` when one child differs, `isDivergent: false` when all children match the header, header-only divergent detection) + 5 source-text tests in `guest-app/tests/api/mrb-14-reschedule-preserves-header.test.ts` (no `checkIn` / `checkOut` / `numNights` write in `handleRescheduleBooking`; the handler queries children via `reservationId`; the header's `actualDateRange` is recomputed; `revenueAllocation` re-stamp on reschedule; pre-MRB-14 docs are byte-equivalent) + 1 MRB-02.x rewrite + 1 admin hydration test in `mrb-12-admin-reservation-affordance.test.ts`. **(2) PR2 — `POST /api/bookings/add-room` endpoint + admin modal (v0.247.0)** — `handleAddRoomToReservation` in `guest-app/server/handlers/bookings.ts` (~700 lines added; new handler appended after `handleRescheduleBooking`). Validates inside the transaction: reservation exists + is pre-arrival (no `checked-in` or `cancelled` children) + target room is `available` + room is not blocked for the header's dates + room is not already claimed by another stay in the same reservation + room type's `maxCapacity` + `maxChildren` + `maxExtraBeds` are respected (the same `requiredExtraBedsFor` math the walkin handler uses). The new child is created via the existing `walkinRoomStayFinancials` chain — same per-line pricing, same per-stream `revenueAllocation` snapshot (MRB-11), same `getLockedManualNightlyRate` for manual-rate reservations. The reservation header is updated in the same transaction: `roomCount += 1`, `activeRoomCount += 1`, `subtotal += newChild.subtotal`, `totalPrice += newChild.totalPrice`, `aggregateRevenueAllocation = sum(children.allocation)` (server-computed via the existing `computeBookingRevenueAllocation`), `actualDateRange` is recomputed (always the same as the header on add-room — `isDivergent` stays `false`). `corporateCodes.usageCount` increments by 1 if the reservation is corporate (per MRB-08's "N rooms = N uses" rule); `vouchers.usageCount` increments by 1 if a `voucherCode` is applied to the new child. The new child's `holdExpiresAt` inherits the header's (a pre-arrival reservation has a unified hold per PEX-01). One `sendBookingTrigger("booking-rescheduled", view)` fires after the commit (the existing reservation-scope view carries the new room; the subject reads `Reservation updated: R-… (N rooms)`). The `requestFingerprint` is a fresh `add-room-${reservationId}-${roomId}-${now}` (the idempotency key — a retry after an uncertain response replays the original commit, never appends a duplicate room). Route registered: `POST /api/bookings/add-room` in `guest-app/server/apiRouter.ts` (staff auth, shares the `bookings-reschedule:30/min/IP` rate bucket). Admin modal in `BookingsPage.tsx`'s "Add room to this reservation" button (gates on `selectedBooking.reservationId && RESCHEDULABLE_STATUSES.includes(selectedBooking.status)`). 11 endpoint tests in `guest-app/tests/api/mrb-14-add-room-endpoint.test.ts` (schema, handler, transaction, email, idempotency) + 2 admin modal tests in `mrb-12-admin-reservation-affordance.test.ts` (button gating + body shape). **(3) PR3 — Per-child dates UI in row + drawer + email + receipt (v0.248.0)** — Bookings table Dates column renders the divergent dates with a "varies by room" badge when `row.listReservationHeader?.actualDateRange?.isDivergent` is true; a new `formatShortDate(value)` helper at `BookingsPage.tsx:116+` uses the existing `toDate` and formats to "MMM D" en-US UTC. Drawer reservation strip gains a third pill row (`data-testid="reservation-strip-actual-range"`) below the Total / Paid / Balance pills showing the actual range when `selectedReservationContext.reservationId` → `reservationsMap.get(...).actualDateRange?.isDivergent`. Email `buildReservationEmailView` adds `actualDateRange` to the view (read from `reservation.actualDateRange`) and per-room `checkIn` / `checkOut` to `roomProjections` (previously absent). Email `bookingRows` reservation branch (line 627, reservation branch at 645) reads `booking.actualDateRange?.isDivergent`; when divergent, the check-in / check-out rows show the actual range + "(varies by room)" instead of the lead booking's dates, and the rooms table cells include the per-room dates. Receipt PDF Stay card gains an "Actual range" line at the reservation level when `receiptReservation?.actualDateRange?.isDivergent` (per-room dates are already shown per-row in the pricing breakdown — a new line prints them inline under each room label when divergent, otherwise the line is omitted so the reservation's shared dates aren't duplicated). 12 source-text tests in `admin-app/src/__tests__/mrb-14-04-per-child-dates-ui.test.ts` (row Dates column reads `isDivergent` as the gate; row renders the badge with the actual range; per-child dates tooltip on the divergent row; drawer strip reads the header + gates on `isDivergent`; drawer pill renders the earliest/latest format + "varies by room" chip; receipt Stay card adds the Actual range row when divergent; receipt pricing breakdown prints per-room dates inline when divergent; email view's `roomProjections` carry per-child `checkIn` / `checkOut`; email view exposes `actualDateRange` at the top level; email `bookingRows` reservation branch detects divergence; email divergent check-in / check-out rows append "(varies by room)"; per-room dates appear inline on each room row when divergent). **(4) MDs updated** — `plan/project/ROADMAP.md` (MRB-14 flipped to ✅ shipped, MRB-14-01..05 all ✅, MRB-15 reference to "add-room adjustments" noted as spec-only with the behavioural emulator follow-up deferred to the Java-emulator env);
